([snjnlsn/snjnlsn-marketplace](https://github.com/snjnlsn/snjnlsn-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/overrides/skills/using-overrides))
- **using-ovnewton**: Connect the Newton physics engine to an Omniverse simulation or ovstage with ovnewton. Use when importing a populated ovstage into Newton, stepping its physics, synchronizing stage edits, or publishing simulation state for Omniverse rendering. ([dannywise093-crypto/NEXORA-AI](https://github.com/dannywise093-crypto/NEXORA-AI/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ovnewton-usage) · 5 copies)
- **using-oxagen**: Always-on routing policy for the Oxagen plugin. Use whenever the user asks a substantive question about the workspace, requests non-trivial changes to artifacts the workspace tracks (code, docs, runbooks, contracts, tickets, incidents), wants memory recall, or coordinates with other agents on the same workspace. Pairs with oxagen-tool-reference (full catalog) and oxagen-dogfooding (write-back). ([macanderson/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/macanderson/claude-code-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/using-oxagen) · ★1)
- **using-pack-fork**: Use when a target repository is already in production with its own real conventions (tech stack, naming, shared components, auth, design system) and you want a pack that captures them, so a later modernize-implement run reuses them instead of guessing. ([riccardomerolla/llm4ts](https://github.com/riccardomerolla/llm4ts/tree/HEAD/skills/using-pack-fork))
- **using-pandastack**: Use at the start of any session — establishes the cognitive contract that pandastack skills must be checked BEFORE any response or action, including clarifying questions. - **using-paprika**: Use when anything in the conversation touches the user's recipes, meal plan, shopping or kitchen — injected at the start of every session, so it never needs invoking by name. ([jeffrichley/paprika](https://github.com/jeffrichley/paprika/tree/HEAD/skills/using-paprika))
